. di "SUMMARY STATISTICS BY COUNTRY (TABLE OA1)"
{res}SUMMARY STATISTICS BY COUNTRY (TABLE OA1)
{txt}
{com}. ********************************************************************************
.  
. use "$data\2_Interim files\country_cleaned.dta", clear
{txt}
{com}.  
. bysort country: egen fin_dev1_av=mean(fin_dev1)
{txt}
{com}. bysort country: egen fin_str2_av=mean(fin_str2)
{txt}
{com}. 
. bysort country: egen cotwo_total_per_gdp_av=mean(cotwo_total_per_gdp)
{txt}
{com}. 
. sort country year
{txt}
{com}. drop if country[_n]==country[_n-1]
{txt}(1,435 observations deleted)

{com}. 
. //
. 
. keep country fin_dev1_av fin_str2_av  cotwo_total_per_gdp_av
{txt}
{com}. 
. replace country = "China" if country == "China (People's Republic of)"
{txt}(0 real changes made)

{com}. replace country = "Macedonia" if country == "Macedonia, FYR"
{txt}(1 real change made)

{com}. 
. foreach v of varlist fin_dev1_av fin_str2_av  cotwo_total_per_gdp_av {c -(}
{txt}  2{com}.     gen `v'_r=string(`v',"%4.3f")
{txt}  3{com}.         destring `v'_r, replace
{txt}  4{com}.         drop `v'
{txt}  5{com}. {c )-}
{txt}fin_dev1_av_r: all characters numeric; {res}replaced {txt}as {res}double
{txt}fin_str2_av_r: all characters numeric; {res}replaced {txt}as {res}double
{txt}cotwo_total_per_gdp_av_r: all characters numeric; {res}replaced {txt}as {res}double
{txt}
{com}. 
. cd "$tables"

. 
. use "$data\2_Interim files\country_sector_cleaned.dta", clear
{txt}
{com}. 
. *Financial development = credit + stocks + bonds; Equity share = stocks / (credit + stocks + bonds)
. label var share_l1 "Sector share"
{txt}
{com}. 
. foreach i of varlist _all {c -(}
{txt}  2{com}.         local a : variable label `i'
{txt}  3{com}.         local a: subinstr local a " cross " " $\times$ "
{txt}  4{com}.         label var `i' "`a'"
{txt}  5{com}. {c )-}
{txt}
{com}. 
. eststo clear
